Theorem 233.1 (Duality Relation)

Statement: Under the Duality Relation assumption on differential structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 309. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 907 with relations in degree 6.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 309 is immediate from the Duality Relation property. For the inductive step, we decompose the differential structure using the Duality Relation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 907. Each subproblem has a solution by the Duality Relation fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 233.2 (Duality Relation)

Theorem 233.4 (Duality Relation)

Statement: For Duality Relation pairs (A,B) in differential theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 562 and the invariant I(A,B) = 39 mod 4.

QED.
